000005: // lookups consistent and allow full Apr–Mar tax year grouping on Calendar.

000007: // Current FY start year (Apr-based UK tax year)
000008: function _currentFYStart(){
000009:   const now=new Date(); const m=now.getMonth(); const y=now.getFullYear();
000010:   return m>=3?y:y-1;
000011: }
000012: 
000013: // Build the full ordered list of 'MMM-YY' keys for a given FY start year
000014: // FY 2025 = APR-25, MAY-25, … DEC-25, JAN-26, FEB-26, MAR-26
000015: function _fyKeys(fyStart){
000016:   const order=['APR','MAY','JUN','JUL','AUG','SEP','OCT','NOV','DEC','JAN','FEB','MAR'];
000017:   return order.map(function(mo){
000018:     const yr = mo==='JAN'||mo==='FEB'||mo==='MAR' ? fyStart+1 : fyStart;
000019:     return mo+'-'+String(yr).slice(-2);
000020:   });
000021: }

000039: // Strip year suffix → base code: 'APR-26' → 'APR'
000040: function keyCode(k){ return (k||'').split('-')[0]; }
000041: // Strip to 2-digit year: 'APR-26' → '26', then full year
000042: function keyYear(k){
000043:   const parts=(k||'').split('-'); if(parts.length<2)return new Date().getFullYear();
000044:   const yy=parseInt(parts[1]||'0'); return yy>=50?1900+yy:2000+yy;
000045: }
000046: // Display name: 'APR-26' → 'April 2026'

000049: // All DB keys that should exist: current FY + previous FY + any FY with data + next FY
000050: // v1.4.2 — repair legacy Sale-N return attribution.
000051: //
000052: // Older builds could leave two different completed resale returns tagged to the
000053: // same sale number (most commonly Sale 1). That makes the P&L engine do exactly
000054: // what the bad data says: charge multiple full refunds against one sale and can
000055: // turn a small fulfilment loss into a huge fictional lifetime loss.
000056: //
000057: // Full returns already contain an immutable `_dateSoldAtReturn` snapshot. When
000058: // there are 2+ full returns with distinct sold dates, chronological sold dates
000059: // are authoritative: first completed sale = Sale 1, next = Sale 2, etc. This is
000060: // safer than refund/log dates because a historical return can be entered later.
000061: // Same-day/ambiguous records are deliberately NOT auto-renumbered.
000062: function _returnSaleSnapshotDate(r){
000063:   const d=r&&r._dateSoldAtReturn;
000064:   return d?String(d).slice(0,10):null;
000065: }
000066: function _repairSaleNoSequenceForItem(it){
000067:   if(!it||!Array.isArray(it.returnHistory))return 0;
000068:   const rh=it.returnHistory;
000069:   const fulls=[];
000070:   rh.forEach(function(r,idx){
000071:     if(r&&(r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')){
000072:       fulls.push({r:r,idx:idx,sold:_returnSaleSnapshotDate(r)});
000073:     }
000074:   });
000075:   if(!fulls.length)return 0;
000076: 
000077:   let touched=0;
000078:   // A single full return is always Sale 1 unless it was explicitly tagged.
000079:   if(fulls.length===1){
000080:     if(fulls[0].r.saleNo==null){fulls[0].r.saleNo=1;touched++;}
000081:   }else{
000082:     const allDated=fulls.every(function(x){return !!x.sold;});
000083:     const uniqueDates=allDated?(new Set(fulls.map(function(x){return x.sold;}))).size:0;
000084:     if(allDated&&uniqueDates===fulls.length){
000085:       const ordered=fulls.slice().sort(function(a,b){
000086:         return a.sold.localeCompare(b.sold)
000087:           ||String(a.r.loggedAt||'').localeCompare(String(b.r.loggedAt||''))
000088:           ||a.idx-b.idx;
000089:       });
000090:       ordered.forEach(function(x,pos){
000091:         const target=pos+1;
000092:         if((Number(x.r.saleNo)||0)!==target){
000093:           x.r.saleNo=target;
000094:           touched++;
000095:         }
000096:       });
000097:     }
000098:   }
000099: 
000100:   // Partials that carry the same immutable sold-date snapshot belong to the
000101:   // same sale as that full return. This only changes an untagged/mistagged
000102:   // partial when the mapping is exact.
000103:   const saleBySoldDate=new Map();
000104:   fulls.forEach(function(x){
000105:     const n=Math.max(1,Number(x.r.saleNo)||1);
000106:     if(x.sold&&!saleBySoldDate.has(x.sold))saleBySoldDate.set(x.sold,n);
000107:   });
000108:   rh.forEach(function(r){
000109:     if(!r||(r.type!=='partial_seller'&&r.type!=='partial_ebay'))return;
000110:     const sold=_returnSaleSnapshotDate(r);
000111:     if(!sold||!saleBySoldDate.has(sold))return;
000112:     const target=saleBySoldDate.get(sold);
000113:     if((Number(r.saleNo)||0)!==target){r.saleNo=target;touched++;}
000114:   });
000115:   return touched;
000116: }

000118: // Launch migration entry point. First repair reliable full-return sequences,
000119: // then retain the old conservative fallback for any genuinely untagged rows
000120: // whose historical snapshots are too old to reconstruct safely.
000121: function _migrateSaleNoTags(){

000123:   try{
000124:     (typeof allDBKeys==='function'?allDBKeys():[]).forEach(function(k){
000125:       (Array.isArray(DB[k])?DB[k]:[]).forEach(function(it){

000127:         let itemTouched=_repairSaleNoSequenceForItem(it);

000130:           .map(function(r){return Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1);});
000131:         const maxFullNo=fullNos.length?Math.max.apply(null,fullNos):1;
000132:         // If the item is currently fully returned, the latest full-return row
000133:         // already represents the current sale. Otherwise the live resale (if
000134:         // any) is the cycle after the latest completed full return.

000136:         it.returnHistory.forEach(function(r){

000139:           if(rd&&logged&&logged>=rd){r.saleNo=currentResaleNo;itemTouched++;}
000140:           else {r.saleNo=1;itemTouched++;}

000151: function allDBKeys(){
000152:   const curFY=_currentFYStart();
000153:   const fySet=new Set([curFY-1, curFY, curFY+1]);
000154:   // Also include any FY that has data already
000155:   Object.keys(DB).forEach(function(k){
000156:     if(k==='trips'||k==='expenses'||k==='_userOwned'||k==='_taxYear')return;
000157:     if(!DB[k]||!DB[k].length)return;
000158:     const code=keyCode(k); const yr=keyYear(k);
000159:     if(!MONTHS.includes(code))return;
000160:     // Determine FY for this key
000161:     const mo=MONTHS.indexOf(code);
000162:     const fy=mo>=3?yr:yr-1;
000163:     fySet.add(fy);

000165:   const keys=[];

000167:     _fyKeys(fy).forEach(function(k){if(!keys.includes(k))keys.push(k);});
000168:   });
000169:   return keys;
000170: }

000194: // Patch A — Item state migration. One-time pass: every item must have a
000195: // canonical `state` field of 'sourced' | 'listed' | 'sold' | 'returned'.
000196: // State is purely for filtering/display in this patch — financial logic
000197: // continues to read existing fields (sold/isReturned/resaleSalePrice etc).
000198: // Derivation rules (in order):
000199: //   active return in returnHistory[] (no resale yet)         → 'returned'
000200: //   has dateSold or resaleSalePrice (and not returned)       → 'sold'
000201: //   otherwise                                                → 'listed'
000202: // Stock items (state='stock') are pre-listing and will only ever exist if
000203: // they were created via the Stock Quick Add flow — never produced by this
000204: // migration (no existing field can imply pre-listing intent).
000205: //
000206: // ALSO: backfill dateSourced for items that don't have it. Anything created
000207: // via the old List Quick Add flow gets dateSourced=dateListed (effectively
000208: // "sourced and listed same day" → 0d time-to-list). Falls back to dateSold
000209: // for ancient entries with neither, ensuring the field is never undefined.
000210: // This matters for the Stock page's "Avg time to list" KPI which would
000211: // otherwise have to skip un-sourced items entirely.
000212: function _migrateItemStates(){
000213:   const _VALID_STATES=['sourced','listed','sold','returned'];
000214:   let touchedState=0, touchedSourced=0, reconciled=0;
000215:   allDBKeys().forEach(function(k){
000216:     (DB[k]||[]).forEach(function(i){
000217:       // E7: remap legacy 'stock' → 'sourced'. Items saved before E6 renamed the
000218:       // pre-listing state carry 'stock', which the DB CHECK constraint rejects.
000219:       // 'stock' is truthy so the !i.state guard below would silently skip it.
000220:       if(i.state==='stock'){
000221:         i.state='sourced';
000222:         touchedState++;
000223:       }
000224:       // Catch null/undefined/empty/any other unrecognised value
000225:       if(!i.state || !_VALID_STATES.includes(i.state)){
000226:         const _hadFullReturn=(i.returnHistory||[]).some(function(r){return r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay';});
000227:         const _activeReturn=i.isReturned===true || (_hadFullReturn && !i.resaleSalePrice);
000228:         if(_activeReturn){ i.state='returned'; }
000229:         else if(i.dateSold || i.resaleSalePrice){ i.state='sold'; }
000230:         else { i.state='listed'; }
000231:         touchedState++;
000232:       }
000233:       // v2.09.4 — Reconcile ghost states left by pre-v2.09.2 duplicates. An item
000234:       // with state='sold' but no dateSold AND no resaleSalePrice AND not
000235:       // returned isn't actually sold — the source item was, the duplicate
000236:       // inherited the state but had its sale fields cleared. Stock Room shows
000237:       // these correctly as listed (its filter ignores state), but the account
000238:       // page and other state-driven UIs read the wrong badge. Correct here.
000239:       // Same pattern for state='returned' without an isReturned/return record.
000240:       if(i.state==='sold' && !i.dateSold && !i.resaleSalePrice && !i.isReturned){
000241:         i.state='listed';
000242:         reconciled++;
000243:       } else if(i.state==='returned' && !i.isReturned){
000244:         const _hadFullReturn=(i.returnHistory||[]).some(function(r){return r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay';});
000245:         if(!_hadFullReturn){
000246:           // No supporting evidence — treat as active. If dateSold survived,
000247:           // it was truly sold before the return got cleared; otherwise listed.
000248:           i.state=(i.dateSold||i.resaleSalePrice)?'sold':'listed';
000249:           reconciled++;
000250:         }
000251:       }
000252:       if(!i.dateSourced){
000253:         i.dateSourced = i.dateListed || i.dateSold || null;
000254:         if(i.dateSourced) touchedSourced++;
000255:       }
000256:     });
000257:   });
000258:   if(reconciled>0)console.info('[RETRADE] v2.09.4 state reconcile: fixed '+reconciled+' ghost-sold/returned item'+(reconciled!==1?'s':''));
000259:   return reconciled;
000260: }

000263: // to infer the correct year. Runs once after loadFromSupabase.
000264: function _migrateBareDBKeys(){
000265:   const curFY=_currentFYStart();
000266:   MONTHS.forEach(function(code){
000267:     if(!DB[code]||!DB[code].length)return;
000268:     const items=DB[code];
000269:     // Group items by their actual year — infer from dateListed or dateSold
000270:     const byYear={};
000271:     items.forEach(function(i){
000272:       const dateStr=i.dateListed||i.dateSold||null;
000273:       let yr=curFY; // fallback: assume current tax year context
000274:       if(dateStr){
000275:         const d=new Date(dateStr);
000276:         if(!isNaN(d)) yr=d.getFullYear();
000277:       } else {
000278:         // No date: use FY logic — Jan/Feb/Mar belong to prev year's tail
000279:         const mo=MONTHS.indexOf(code);
000280:         yr = mo>=3 ? curFY : curFY+1; // APR-DEC → curFY; JAN-MAR → curFY+1 (next calendar yr)
000281:         // But only if we're actually past APR — if it's early year, shift back
000282:         if(mo<3) yr=curFY; // conservative: keep in current tracked year
000283:       }
000284:       const yy=String(yr).slice(-2);
000285:       const key=code+'-'+yy;
000286:       if(!byYear[key])byYear[key]=[];
000287:       byYear[key].push(i);
000288:     });
000289:     Object.keys(byYear).forEach(function(k){
000290:       if(!DB[k])DB[k]=[];
000291:       byYear[k].forEach(function(i){ if(!DB[k].find(function(x){return x.id===i.id;}))DB[k].push(i); });
000292:     });
000293:     delete DB[code];

## Window 6: lines 2733-2927
002733: async function _uploadItemPhoto(itemId,file){
002734:   if(!file)return null;
002735:   // Preview mode has no authenticated Storage identity; keep a temporary local
002736:   // preview instead of pretending it is cloud-persistent.
002737:   if(!_currentUserId){
002738:     if(PREVIEW_MODE_ENABLED)return {path:null,url:await _fileToDataUrl(file),previewOnly:true};
002739:     throw new Error('Sign in before uploading a photo.');
002740:   }
002741:   const packed=await _compressItemPhoto(file);
002742:   const safeId=String(itemId||'item').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'_');
002743:   const path=_currentUserId+'/'+safeId+'/'+Date.now()+'.'+packed.ext;
002744:   const res=await _sbCall(()=>_sb.storage.from(RETRADE_PHOTO_BUCKET).upload(path,packed.blob,{contentType:packed.contentType,upsert:false,cacheControl:'31536000'}));
002745:   if(res.error)throw new Error('Photo upload failed: '+res.error.message);
002746:   return {path:path,url:await _signedPhotoUrl(path,true),previewOnly:false};
002747: }

002751: function _rowToItem(row, parts, returns){
002752:   const item = {
002753:     id:              row.id,
002754:     gid:             row.gid || null,
002755:     month:           row.month,
002756:     item:            row.item,
002757:     category:        row.category || null,
002758:     location:        row.location || null,
002759:     source:          row.source || null,
002760:     dateListed:      row.date_listed || null,
002761:     dateSold:        row.date_sold || null,
002762:     salePrice:       Number(row.sale_price) || 0,
002763:     postage:         Number(row.postage) || 0,
002764:     costPrice:       Number(row.cost_price) || 0,
002765:     shippingCost:    Number(row.shipping_cost) || 0,
002766:     packagingCost:   Number(row.packaging_cost) || 0,
002767:     promoPercent:    Number(row.promo_percent) || 0,
002768:     notes:           row.notes || '',

002770:     photo:           null,
002771:     isReturned:      !!row.is_returned,
002772:     _postageMode:    row.postage_mode || 'simple',
002773:     _sdRateIdx:      row.sd_rate_idx != null ? row.sd_rate_idx : 1,
002774:     _sdBuyerPays:    row.sd_buyer_pays === true,
002775:     resaleSalePrice:    row.resale_sale_price ? Number(row.resale_sale_price) : null,
002776:     resaleDateSold:     row.resale_date_sold || null,
002777:     resalePlatform:     row.resale_platform || null,
002778:     salePlatform:       row.sale_platform || null,   // v2.19.33 — Sale 1's own platform
002779:     refreshHistory:     (function(){try{return row.refresh_history?JSON.parse(row.refresh_history):[];}catch(e){return [];}})(),
002780:     _lastRelistAt:      row.last_relist_at || null,
002781:     _lastRefreshAt:     row.last_refresh_at || null,

002787:     state:           row.state || null,
002788:       scrappedAt:      row.scrapped_at || null,
002789:       scrapReason:     row.scrap_reason || null,
002790:       scrapNote:       row.scrap_note || null,
002791:       supplierRefund:  row.supplier_refund != null ? Number(row.supplier_refund) : null,
002792:       supplierRefundDate: row.supplier_refund_date || null,
002793:       bundleId:        row.bundle_id || null,

003145: // Fills the snapshot on legacy return entries created before the column existed.
003146: // Strategy per item:
003147: //   1. If r._dateSoldAtReturn already set → skip
003148: //   2. Else if i.dateSold present → use that (most cases)
003149: //   3. Else (full return wiped dateSold) → use the FULL return's loggedAt as
003150: //      last-resort approximation. The original sale was at-or-before the return
003151: //      logged date; for backdating purposes loggedAt is the best available proxy.
003152: // Only persists if something changed, so subsequent loads are zero-write.
003153: async function _backfillDateSoldAtReturn(){
003154:   // One-shot guard: once every return entry in this account has _dateSoldAtReturn
003155:   // set, we write a localStorage flag so subsequent loads skip the scan entirely.
003156:   // Key is user-agnostic (preview mode also benefits) — safe to share per-browser.
003157:   const _BACKFILL_KEY='retrade_backfill_dsar_v1';
003158:   if(localStorage.getItem(_BACKFILL_KEY)==='1')return;
003159:   let dirty=0;
003160:   allDBKeys().forEach(function(k){
003161:     if(k==='trips'||k==='expenses'||k==='_userOwned'||k==='_taxYear')return;
003162:     (DB[k]||[]).forEach(function(i){
003163:       const rh=i.returnHistory||[];
003164:       if(rh.length===0)return;
003165:       const fullEntry=rh.find(r=>r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay');
003166:       const fallbackForFullReturn=fullEntry?(fullEntry.loggedAt||null):null;
003167:       rh.forEach(function(r){
003168:         if(r._dateSoldAtReturn)return; // already set, skip
003169:         const filled=i.dateSold||fallbackForFullReturn||null;
003170:         if(filled){
003171:           r._dateSoldAtReturn=filled;
003172:           dirty++;
003173:         }
003174:       });
003175:     });
003176:   });
003177:   if(dirty>0){
003178:     try{
003179:       // Re-save every item that has returnHistory so the column gets pushed up
003180:       // for ALL of them in a single sync. saveDB's debounced diff engine will
003181:       // batch this into one Supabase round trip.
003182:       await saveDB();
003183:     }catch(e){
003184:       console.warn('[backfill] save failed — values are correct in memory but not persisted:',e);
003185:     }
003186:   }
003187:   // Mark done regardless of whether anything was filled — if dirty=0 it means
003188:   // all entries were already set, so future loads can skip immediately.
003189:   try{ localStorage.setItem(_BACKFILL_KEY,'1'); }catch(e){}
003190: }

003192: // ── Missing-column resilience (v2.19.38) ────────────────────────────────────
003193: // A column the current app writes but the DB hasn't migrated yet (sale_platform,
003194: // resale_listing_fee, the *_at_relist return snapshots, sourcing_runs.ended_at_iso,
003195: // …) makes PostgREST reject the WHOLE upsert. Before this, that threw, _persistChanges
003196: // swallowed it as a quiet "schema mismatch" console.warn, and the row never reached
003197: // the cloud — so on reload the old row came back: sold reverted to listed, new items
003198: // vanished, and a return that hit a missing column was lost (double-counting the item).
003199: // These helpers strip the offending column(s) and retry so the row STILL saves (minus
003200: // the un-migrated field) and warn ONCE so the migration gets run. Generalises the
003201: // pre-existing account_* / trip-expenses one-off fallbacks.
003202: function _missingColumnFromError(err){
003203:   const m=(err&&err.message)||'';
003204:   let mm=m.match(/Could not find the '([^']+)' column/i);                            // PGRST204
003205:   if(mm) return mm[1];
003206:   mm=m.match(/column "?([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)"?(?: of relation "[^"]+")? does not exist/i);  // 42703
003207:   if(mm) return mm[1];
003208:   return null;
003209: }

016115: function _applyIncludedReconcile(existing,orphan,total,dateSold,platform,target){
016116:   const all=existing.slice();all.push(orphan);
016117:   // Use the values the records already carry as the allocation signal. This
016118:   // mirrors normal bundle creation and avoids the old bug that weighted revenue
016119:   // by COST, which had no relationship to what the buyer paid.
016120:   const weights=all.map(function(i){return Math.max(0,Number(i.salePrice)||0);});
016121:   const shares=_bundleSplit(total,weights.some(function(v){return v>0;})?weights:all.map(function(){return 1;}));
016122:   _prepareHistoricalSaleItem(orphan,dateSold,platform,target);
016123:   all.forEach(function(i,idx){i.salePrice=shares[idx];});
016124:   const b=_ensureBundleForReconcile(all,target,total,dateSold);
016125:   return {bundleId:b.bundleId,bundleRef:b.bundleRef,total:+Number(total).toFixed(2),shares:shares};
016126: }
016127: function _applyAdditionalReconcile(existing,orphan,additional,dateSold,platform,target){

017355: function _canScrap(i){

017361:   return !((i.dateSold||i.resaleSalePrice)&&!i.isReturned);

018640: // Custom Range presets — writes ISO dates into the two date inputs.
018641: function _setRangePreset(kind){
018642:   const fromEl=document.getElementById('dl-range-from');
018643:   const toEl=document.getElementById('dl-range-to');
018644:   if(!fromEl||!toEl)return;
018645:   const today=new Date();
018646:   const iso=function(d){
018647:     const y=d.getFullYear(), m=(d.getMonth()+1), day=d.getDate();
018648:     return y+'-'+(m<10?'0':'')+m+'-'+(day<10?'0':'')+day;
018649:   };
018650:   let from=null,to=iso(today);
018651:   if(kind==='mtd'){
018652:     from=iso(new Date(today.getFullYear(),today.getMonth(),1));
018653:   } else if(kind==='lastMonth'){
018654:     const first=new Date(today.getFullYear(),today.getMonth()-1,1);
018655:     const last=new Date(today.getFullYear(),today.getMonth(),0);
018656:     from=iso(first); to=iso(last);
018657:   } else if(kind==='last30'){
018658:     const d=new Date(today); d.setDate(d.getDate()-29); from=iso(d);
018659:   } else if(kind==='last90'){
018660:     const d=new Date(today); d.setDate(d.getDate()-89); from=iso(d);
018661:   } else if(kind==='ytd'){
018662:     // Use UK tax year start (6 Apr) if we're past it, else previous 6 Apr
018663:     const y=today.getFullYear();
018664:     const fyStart=(today.getMonth()>2||(today.getMonth()===3&&today.getDate()>=6))
018665:       ? new Date(y,3,6) : new Date(y-1,3,6);
018666:     from=iso(fyStart);
018667:   }
018668:   if(from){fromEl.value=from; toEl.value=to;}
018669: }

018756: function _isValidStoredDate(v){

020303: // v1.4.6 — Canonical lifecycle state machine. All primary item actions use
020304: // this single resolver so List / Mark Sold / Return / Relist do not drift apart.
020305: function _itemLifecycleState(i){
020306:   if(!i)return 'missing';
020307:   if(i.scrappedAt)return 'disposed';
020308:   if(i.isReturned)return 'returned';
020309:   if(i.resaleSalePrice||i.dateSold)return 'sold';
020310:   if(i.state==='sourced')return 'sourced';
020311:   return 'listed';
020312: }
020313: function _lifecycleCan(i,action){
020314:   const s=_itemLifecycleState(i);
020315:   if(action==='list')return s==='sourced';
020316:   if(action==='markSold')return s==='listed';
020317:   if(action==='return')return s==='sold';
020318:   if(action==='relist')return s==='returned';
020319:   if(action==='moveUnlisted')return s==='returned';
020320:   return false;
020321: }
020322: function _lifecycleActionSummary(i){
020323:   const s=_itemLifecycleState(i);
020324:   return {state:s,list:_lifecycleCan(i,'list'),markSold:_lifecycleCan(i,'markSold'),returnItem:_lifecycleCan(i,'return'),relist:_lifecycleCan(i,'relist'),moveUnlisted:_lifecycleCan(i,'moveUnlisted')};
020325: }

020333:   const today=new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];

020734: function duplicateItem(m,id,context){

020739:   copy.gid='R-'+String(getNextGID()).padStart(4,'0');

020741:   // v2.09.2 — Force a clean "active listing" state. Previously only dateSold /
020742:   // grossProfit / isReturned were cleared, so duplicating a sold-then-relisted
020743:   // item left resaleSalePrice / resaleDateSold intact — the Account page's
020744:   // sold-filter (i.state==='sold' || !!i.resaleSalePrice) then treated the copy
020745:   // as a sale even though the Stock Room showed it correctly as listed.
020746:   copy.state='listed';

020748:   copy.resaleSalePrice=null;copy.resaleDateSold=null;copy.resaleGrossProfit=null;
020749:   // v2.19.40 — a clone starts as a fresh Sale 1, so it must not inherit the
020750:   // original's per-sale platforms. soldOnPlatform (sale-time platform) and
020751:   // resalePlatform (sale-2 platform) were left set, so a clone of an item sold
020752:   // on a non-default platform was mispriced off that stale platform's fees.
020753:   copy.soldOnPlatform=null;copy.resalePlatform=null;
020754:   // v2.19.26 — the clone inherits resaleListingFee, which calcGrossProfit now
020755:   // charges unconditionally. Left uncleared the duplicate would be billed for
020756:   // the ORIGINAL's relist. Clear it, then stamp a fresh sale-1 fee: a
020757:   // duplicate is a new listing and eBay bills it as one.
020758:   copy.resaleListingFee=0;

020928: function confirmRelist(m,id){
020929:   const i=(DB[m]||[]).find(x=>x.id===id);
020930:   if(!i)return;
020931:   const price=parseFloat(document.getElementById('relist-price').value)||i.salePrice||0;
020932:   const promoInput=parseFloat(document.getElementById('relist-promo').value)||0;
020933:   // E3 P1 #3 — Capture sale 2 packaging from the relist modal so it isn't
020934:   // silently inherited from sale 1. The input pre-fills with i.packagingCost
020935:   // (the sale 1 value) so the user can adjust if they used a different
020936:   // mailer for the relist. Empty / NaN falls back to sale 1's value.
020937:   const _pkgRaw=document.getElementById('relist-packaging');
020938:   const newPackaging=_pkgRaw&&_pkgRaw.value!==''?(parseFloat(_pkgRaw.value)||0):(i.packagingCost||0);
020939:   const condition=window._relistCondition||'working';
020940:   const postMode=window._relistPostMode||'simple';
020941:   // Per-resale platform — the relist can be on a different platform than sale 1
020942:   // (e.g. sold on eBay private, returned, relisted on eBay Business). The resale
020943:   // fee in calcGrossProfit reads i.resalePlatform so sale-2 fees are correct.
020944:   const _relistPlatEl=document.getElementById('relist-platform');
020945:   const _newRelistPlatform=(_relistPlatEl&&_relistPlatEl.value&&PLATFORMS[_relistPlatEl.value])?_relistPlatEl.value:(i.resalePlatform||_itemPlatform(i));
020946:   if(_newRelistPlatform&&PLATFORMS[_newRelistPlatform]){
020947:     i.resalePlatform=_newRelistPlatform;
020948:     // The relisted item is now an ACTIVE listing on the chosen platform, so its
020949:     // live P&L must use that platform's fees — not sale 1's. Update the item's
020950:     // own platform and clear the prior soldOnPlatform so _itemPlatform resolves
020951:     // to the new one. resalePlatform is also kept for the resale fee path.
020952:     i.defaultPlatform=_relistPlatEl.value;
020953:     i.soldOnPlatform=null;
020954:   }
020955:   // E3 P0 #1 — Snapshot sale 1 economics into the latest returnHistory entry
020956:   // BEFORE we overwrite shipping/postage/sdRate/promo with relist values.
020957:   // Without this, the original sale's costs are destroyed and calcGrossProfit
020958:   // ends up double-counting relist shipping (once as i.shippingCost, once as
020959:   // resaleShippingCost) when the resale completes. _shippingCostAtRelist is
020960:   // the marker name — calcGrossProfit reads these on the resold-after-return
020961:   // path. Items that were relisted before this fix don't have the snapshot;
020962:   // they fall back to the live (now-overwritten) values, so older relisted
020963:   // items are still slightly wrong but new relists from this point are correct.
020964:   const _relistReturnEntry=(i.returnHistory||[]).slice().reverse().find(function(r){return (r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')&&!r._relistedAt;})||null;
020965:   if(_relistReturnEntry){
020966:     if(_relistReturnEntry._shippingCostAtRelist===undefined)_relistReturnEntry._shippingCostAtRelist=i.shippingCost||0;
020967:     if(_relistReturnEntry._postageAtRelist===undefined)_relistReturnEntry._postageAtRelist=i.postage||0;
020968:     if(_relistReturnEntry._packagingCostAtRelist===undefined)_relistReturnEntry._packagingCostAtRelist=i.packagingCost||0;
020969:     if(_relistReturnEntry._promoPercentAtRelist===undefined)_relistReturnEntry._promoPercentAtRelist=i.promoPercent||0;
020970:     if(_relistReturnEntry._sdRateIdxAtRelist===undefined)_relistReturnEntry._sdRateIdxAtRelist=(i._sdRateIdx!=null?i._sdRateIdx:1);
020971:     if(_relistReturnEntry._sdBuyerPaysAtRelist===undefined)_relistReturnEntry._sdBuyerPaysAtRelist=!!i._sdBuyerPays;
020972:     if(_relistReturnEntry._salePriceAtRelist===undefined)_relistReturnEntry._salePriceAtRelist=i.salePrice||0;
020973:   }
020974:   // If the return belongs to Sale 2+, that completed failed resale is now
020975:   // fully frozen on its returnHistory row. Clear the single live resale slot so
020976:   // the relisted unit is genuinely active again and can become Sale 3/4/etc.
020977:   // Sale 1 fields are never cleared because they remain the item's original
020978:   // acquisition/sale anchor.
020979:   if(_relistReturnEntry && (Number(_relistReturnEntry.saleNo)||1)>=2){
020980:     i.resaleSalePrice=null;
020981:     i.resaleDateSold=null;
020982:     i.resalePlatform=null;
020983:     i.resalePostage=null;
020984:     i.resaleShippingCost=null;
020985:     i.resalePackagingCost=null;
020986:     i.resalePromoPercent=null;
020987:     i.resaleGrossProfit=null;
020988:     i.resaleBundleId=null;i.resaleBundleRef=null;i.resaleBundleTotal=null;
020989:     // The completed Sale-N slot was cleared above; restore the NEW listing's
020990:     // platform so Sale N+1 does not silently fall back to the previous/default one.
020991:     i.resalePlatform=_newRelistPlatform;
020992:   }
020993:   // Apply postage settings
020994:   i._postageMode=postMode;
020995:   if(postMode==='simple'){
020996:     let sdIdx=parseInt(document.getElementById('relist-sd-rate').value);if(isNaN(sdIdx))sdIdx=1;
020997:     const buyerPays=!document.getElementById('relist-sd-free-postage').checked;
020998:     const rateCost=_sdRateFor(sdIdx,price);
020999:     i._sdRateIdx=sdIdx;
021000:     i._sdBuyerPays=buyerPays;
021001:     i.shippingCost=rateCost;
021002:     i.postage=buyerPays?rateCost:0;
021003:   } else {
021004:     i.shippingCost=parseFloat(document.getElementById('relist-shipping-cost').value)||0;
021005:     i.postage=parseFloat(document.getElementById('relist-postage').value)||0;
021006:   }
021007:   // Restore to active stock
021008:   i.isReturned=false;
021009:   i.state='listed';
021010:   // v2.19.26 — a relist is a second billable listing. Stamped here, after the
021011:   // resalePlatform/defaultPlatform assignment above, so a return-then-relist
021012:   // onto a DIFFERENT platform is priced on the platform it actually went to.
021013:   const _thisRelistFee=_listingFeeFor(i.resalePlatform||_itemPlatform(i));
021014:   _stampRelistFee(i);
021015:   i.dateSold=null;
021016:   i.salePrice=price;
021017:   i.promoPercent=promoInput/100;
021018:   i.packagingCost=newPackaging;
021019:   i.condition=condition==='working'?undefined:'spares';
021020:   i.grossProfit=null;
021021:   // Log relist in returnHistory for audit trail
021022:   if(_relistReturnEntry){
021023:     const relistDate=document.getElementById('relist-date').value||new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
021024:     _relistReturnEntry._relistedAt=relistDate;
021025:     _relistReturnEntry._relistCondition=condition;
021026:     _relistReturnEntry._platformAtRelist=i.resalePlatform||_itemPlatform(i);
021027:     _relistReturnEntry._listingFeeAtRelist=_thisRelistFee;
021028:     // v2.15.0 — register the relist in the maintenance log. Two reasons:
021029:     //  1. The item-page relist COUNTER only reads refreshHistory, so a
021030:     //     relist-after-return never registered. That's why it was being logged
021031:     //     manually as well — which produced a second "Relisted" timeline row
021032:     //     for one real event (the reported double-log).
021033:     //  2. _effectiveListDate resets the freshness clock from _lastRelistAt.
021034:     //     Without this, a relisted item kept its ORIGINAL dateListed age and
021035:     //     drifted into the wrong Stock Room aging bucket.
021036:     // _fromReturn marks this entry as owned by the returnHistory _relistedAt
021037:     // record: the timeline skips it and renders the richer _relistedAt row
021038:     // (which carries condition + price), so one relist stays one row.
021039:     if(!i.refreshHistory)i.refreshHistory=[];
021040:     i.refreshHistory.push({type:'relist',date:relistDate,_fromReturn:true});
021041:     i._lastRelistAt=relistDate;
021042:   }
021043:   saveDB();
021044:   window._relistCondition=null;
021045:   window._relistPostMode=null;
021046:   closePanel();
021047:   toast('Relisted as '+(condition==='spares'?'Spares / Repairs':'Working'));
021048:   const cur=document.querySelector('.page.on').id;
021049:   if(cur==='p-item')renderItemPage(m,id);
021050:   else if(cur==='p-monthly')renderMonth();
021051:   else if(cur==='p-stock')renderStock();
021052:   else renderSummary();
021053: }

021055: function _currentResaleSaleNo(i){
021056:   // Sale 1 uses the base fields. Every later completed sale reuses the live
021057:   // `resale*` slot, while its full-return record freezes that cycle in
021058:   // returnHistory. The most recent relisted full return therefore tells us
021059:   // which numbered resale is currently live (Sale 2, 3, 4, ...).
021060:   const relistedFull=(i&&i.returnHistory||[]).filter(function(r){
021061:     return (r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')&&r._relistedAt;
021062:   });
021063:   let max=1;
021064:   relistedFull.forEach(function(r){max=Math.max(max,Number(r.saleNo)||1);});
021065:   return Math.max(2,max+1);
021066: }
021067: function _currentSaleForReturn(i){
021068:   const hasResale=!!(i&&i.resaleSalePrice);
021069:   const saleNo=hasResale?_currentResaleSaleNo(i):1;
021070:   const isResale=saleNo>=2;
021071:   const price=Math.max(0,Number(isResale?i.resaleSalePrice:i.salePrice)||0);
021072:   const date=(isResale?i.resaleDateSold:i.dateSold)||null;
021073:   const postage=Math.max(0,Number(isResale?(i.resalePostage!=null?i.resalePostage:i.postage):i.postage)||0);
021074:   const platformId=isResale?(i.resalePlatform||_itemPlatform(i)):_s1Platform(i);
021075:   const promoPercent=Math.max(0,Number(isResale?i.resalePromoPercent:i.promoPercent)||0);
021076:   const shippingCost=Math.max(0,Number(isResale?(i.resaleShippingCost!=null?i.resaleShippingCost:i.shippingCost):i.shippingCost)||0);
021077:   const packagingCost=Math.max(0,Number(isResale?(i.resalePackagingCost!=null?i.resalePackagingCost:i.packagingCost):i.packagingCost)||0);
021078:   let listingFee=Math.max(0,Number(isResale?0:i.listingFee)||0);
021079:   if(isResale){
021080:     const prior=((i.returnHistory||[]).slice().reverse().find(function(r){return (r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')&&(Number(r.saleNo)||1)===saleNo-1&&r._relistedAt;}));
021081:     if(prior&&prior._listingFeeAtRelist!=null)listingFee=Math.max(0,Number(prior._listingFeeAtRelist)||0);
021082:     else listingFee=Math.max(0,Number(i.resaleListingFee)||0); // live Sale 2/3/4+ fallback
021083:   }
021084:   const bundleId=isResale?(i.resaleBundleId||null):(i.bundleId||null);
021085:   const bundleRef=isResale?(i.resaleBundleRef||''):(i.bundleRef||'');
021086:   const bundleTotal=Number(isResale?i.resaleBundleTotal:i.bundleTotal)||0;
021087:   return {saleNo:saleNo,is2:isResale,price:price,date:date,postage:postage,platformId:platformId,promoPercent:promoPercent,shippingCost:shippingCost,packagingCost:packagingCost,listingFee:listingFee,bundleId:bundleId,bundleRef:bundleRef,bundleTotal:bundleTotal};
021088: }

021140: function saveReturn(m,id){
021141:   const i=(DB[m]||[]).find(x=>x.id===id);
021142:   if(!i)return;
021143:   const type=document.getElementById('rtn-type').value;
021144:   const refundAmount=parseFloat(document.getElementById('rtn-refund').value)||0;
021145:   const returnDate=document.getElementById('rtn-date').value;
021146:   const returnPostage=parseFloat(document.getElementById('rtn-postage').value)||0;
021147:   const notes=document.getElementById('rtn-notes').value.trim();
021148: 
021149:   // Bind the return to the currently completed sale. All snapshots come from
021150:   // one helper so Sale 2 can never accidentally read Sale 1 economics.
021151:   const cur=_currentSaleForReturn(i);
021152:   const _saleNo=cur.saleNo;
021153:   const entry={
021154:     loggedAt:returnDate,
021155:     type:type,
021156:     refundAmount:refundAmount,
021157:     returnPostage:returnPostage,
021158:     notes:notes,
021159:     saleNo:_saleNo,
021160:     _salePriceAtReturn:cur.price,
021161:     _dateSoldAtReturn:cur.date,
021162:     _postageAtReturn:cur.postage,
021163:     _platformAtReturn:cur.platformId,
021164:     _promoPercentAtReturn:cur.promoPercent,
021165:     _shippingCostAtReturn:cur.shippingCost,
021166:     _packagingCostAtReturn:cur.packagingCost,
021167:     _listingFeeAtReturn:cur.listingFee,
021168:     _bundleIdAtReturn:cur.bundleId||undefined,
021169:     _bundleRefAtReturn:cur.bundleRef||undefined,
021170:     _bundleTotalAtReturn:cur.bundleTotal||undefined
021171:   };
021172:   // For Sale 2+ freeze the live resale fulfilment now. These fields already
021173:   // round-trip through item_returns and let a later relist clear the live
021174:   // resale slot without losing the failed cycle's economics.
021175:   if(_saleNo>=2){
021176:     entry._salePriceAtRelist=cur.price;
021177:     entry._postageAtRelist=cur.postage;
021178:     entry._shippingCostAtRelist=Number(i.resaleShippingCost||0);
021179:     entry._packagingCostAtRelist=Number(i.resalePackagingCost||0);
021180:     entry._promoPercentAtRelist=Number(i.resalePromoPercent||0);
021181:   }
021182: 
021183:   if(!i.returnHistory)i.returnHistory=[];
021184: 
021185:   // A full refund supersedes adjustments for THIS sale only. Preserve the
021186:   // previous sale cycle's history (e.g. Sale 1 partials/full return when Sale 2
021187:   // is later returned). Also replace any duplicate full return on this sale.
021188:   if(type==='full_seller'||type==='full_ebay'){
021189:     i.returnHistory=i.returnHistory.filter(function(r){
021190:       const rs=Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1);
021191:       return rs!==_saleNo;
021192:     });
021193:   }
021194: 
021195:   i.returnHistory.push(entry);
021196: 
021197:   // Mark as fully returned only for full refund types
021198:   if(type==='full_seller'||type==='full_ebay'){
021199:     i.isReturned=true;
021200:     i.state='returned';
021201:     i.dateSold=null; // remove from sold — back to stock
021202:   }
021203:   saveDB();
021204:   toast('Return logged');
021205:   const _activePage=document.querySelector('.page.on');
021206:   const _activeId=_activePage?_activePage.id:'p-summary';
021207:   closePanel();
021208:   if(_activeId==='p-item'){
021209:     // Already on the item page — re-render in place after panel slides out.
021210:     // Do NOT call openItemPage('p-item') or exitItemPage()'s back button breaks.
021211:     setTimeout(function(){ renderItemPage(m,id); }, 340);
021212:   } else {
021213:     // Coming from a list page — navigate to item page after panel clears.
021214:     setTimeout(function(){ openItemPage(m,id,_activeId); }, 340);
021215:   }
021216: }
021217: 
021218: function _removeReturnEntry(m,id,rtnIdx){
021219:   const i=(DB[m]||[]).find(function(x){return x.id===id;});
021220:   if(!i||!Array.isArray(i.returnHistory))return null;
021221:   const r=i.returnHistory[rtnIdx];
021222:   if(!r)return null;
021223:   const saleNo=Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1);
021224:   const isFull=(r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay');
021225: 
021226:   // Every full return is the structural bridge to the NEXT listing cycle.
021227:   // Never let a user delete Sale N's return while its relist or a later sale
021228:   // still exists — that would create Sale N+1 with no originating return.
021229:   const _laterFrozen=(i.returnHistory||[]).some(function(r2){
021230:     return r2!==r&&(r2.type==='full_seller'||r2.type==='full_ebay')&&Math.max(1,Number(r2.saleNo)||1)>saleNo;
021231:   });
021232:   const _liveNo=i.resaleSalePrice?_currentResaleSaleNo(i):null;
021233:   const _laterLive=!!(_liveNo&&_liveNo>saleNo);
021234:   if(isFull&&(r._relistedAt||_laterFrozen||_laterLive)){
021235:     const _later=_laterFrozen||_laterLive;
021236:     toast(_later
021237:       ?'Undo the later sale/relist first, then remove Sale '+saleNo+' return.'
021238:       :'Undo the relist first, then remove Sale '+saleNo+' return.','error');
021239:     return null;
021240:   }
021241: 
021242:   i.returnHistory.splice(rtnIdx,1);
021243:   const stillFullyReturned=i.returnHistory.some(function(r2){
021244:     return (r2.type==='full_seller'||r2.type==='full_ebay')&&!r2._relistedAt;
021245:   });
021246:   if(!stillFullyReturned&&i.isReturned){
021247:     i.isReturned=false;
021248:     i.state='sold';
021249:     if(saleNo===1){
021250:       // Sale 1 full return cleared dateSold; restore its original date.
021251:       if(!i.dateSold)i.dateSold=r._dateSoldAtReturn||null;
021252:     }else{
021253:       // Sale 2 remains represented by resaleSalePrice/resaleDateSold. Never
021254:       // copy the resale date into dateSold — that field belongs to Sale 1.
021255:       i.dateSold=null;
021256:     }
021257:   }
021258:   saveDB();
021259:   return r;
021260: }
021261: 
021262: // v1.4.6 — Dependency-aware return undo. If the only later structural step
021263: // is this return's relist, undo both atomically and restore Sale N as sold.
021264: function _dropReturnOwnedRelistMaintenance(i,r){
021265:   if(!i||!r||!r._relistedAt||!Array.isArray(i.refreshHistory))return;
021266:   const d=String(r._relistedAt).slice(0,10);
021267:   for(let k=i.refreshHistory.length-1;k>=0;k--){
021268:     const e=i.refreshHistory[k];
021269:     if(e&&e.type==='relist'&&e._fromReturn&&String(e.date||'').slice(0,10)===d){i.refreshHistory.splice(k,1);break;}
021270:   }
021271:   const prior=i.refreshHistory.slice().reverse().find(function(e){return e&&e.type==='relist';});
021272:   i._lastRelistAt=prior?prior.date:null;
021273: }
021274: function _restoreCompletedSaleFromReturn(i,r){
021275:   const saleNo=Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1);
021276:   const num=function(v,f){return v!=null?(Number(v)||0):(Number(f)||0);};
021277:   if(saleNo===1){
021278:     i.salePrice=num(r._salePriceAtReturn,i.salePrice);i.dateSold=r._dateSoldAtReturn||i.dateSold||null;
021279:     i.postage=num(r._postageAtReturn,i.postage);i.shippingCost=num(r._shippingCostAtReturn,i.shippingCost);
021280:     i.packagingCost=num(r._packagingCostAtReturn,i.packagingCost);i.promoPercent=num(r._promoPercentAtReturn,i.promoPercent);
021281:     if(r._platformAtReturn)i.soldOnPlatform=r._platformAtReturn;
021282:     i.resaleSalePrice=null;i.resaleDateSold=null;i.resaleGrossProfit=null;i.resalePostage=null;i.resaleShippingCost=null;i.resalePackagingCost=null;i.resalePromoPercent=null;
021283:   }else{
021284:     i.resaleSalePrice=num(r._salePriceAtReturn,r._salePriceAtRelist);i.resaleDateSold=r._dateSoldAtReturn||null;
021285:     i.resalePostage=num(r._postageAtReturn,r._postageAtRelist);i.resaleShippingCost=num(r._shippingCostAtReturn,r._shippingCostAtRelist);
021286:     i.resalePackagingCost=num(r._packagingCostAtReturn,r._packagingCostAtRelist);i.resalePromoPercent=num(r._promoPercentAtReturn,r._promoPercentAtRelist);
021287:     i.resaleListingFee=num(r._listingFeeAtReturn,i.resaleListingFee);i.resalePlatform=r._platformAtReturn||i.resalePlatform||_itemPlatform(i);
021288:     i.salePrice=i.resaleSalePrice;i.postage=i.resalePostage;i.shippingCost=i.resaleShippingCost;i.packagingCost=i.resalePackagingCost;i.promoPercent=i.resalePromoPercent;
021289:   }
021290:   i.isReturned=false;i.state='sold';i.condition=undefined;i.grossProfit=calcGrossProfit(i);
021291: }
021292: async function undoReturnCascade(m,id,rtnIdx){
021293:   const i=(DB[m]||[]).find(function(x){return x.id===id;});
021294:   const r=i&&Array.isArray(i.returnHistory)?i.returnHistory[rtnIdx]:null;if(!r)return;
021295:   const saleNo=Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1);const isFull=r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay';
021296:   if(!isFull||!r._relistedAt)return undoReturnEntry(m,id,rtnIdx);
021297:   const laterReturn=(i.returnHistory||[]).some(function(x){return x!==r&&(x.type==='full_seller'||x.type==='full_ebay')&&Math.max(1,Number(x.saleNo)||1)>saleNo;});
021298:   const liveSaleNo=i.resaleSalePrice?_currentResaleSaleNo(i):0;
021299:   if(laterReturn||(liveSaleNo&&liveSaleNo>saleNo)){toast('A later sale exists. Undo the newest sale/return first so the lifecycle stays intact.','error');return;}
021300:   const ok=await showConfirm('Undo Sale '+saleNo+' return + relist?','This removes the Sale '+saleNo+' refund and its dependent relist for Sale '+(saleNo+1)+', then restores Sale '+saleNo+' as sold. Cashflow and lifetime P&L will recalculate from the corrected history.',{okLabel:'Undo both',danger:false});
021301:   if(!ok)return;
021302:   _dropReturnOwnedRelistMaintenance(i,r);_restoreCompletedSaleFromReturn(i,r);i.returnHistory.splice(rtnIdx,1);saveDB();renderItemPage(m,id);toast('Return + relist undone — Sale '+saleNo+' restored');
021303: }

021318: // Undo the most recent full return → move the item back to Sold. Prominent
021319: // counterpart to the timeline "✕ undo"; completes the per-state "go back".
021320: function undoLastReturn(m,id){
021321:   const i=(DB[m]||[]).find(function(x){return x.id===id;});
021322:   if(!i||!i.returnHistory||!i.returnHistory.length)return;
021323:   let idx=-1;
021324:   for(let k=i.returnHistory.length-1;k>=0;k--){
021325:     const r=i.returnHistory[k];
021326:     if((r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')&&!r._relistedAt){idx=k;break;}
021327:   }
021328:   if(idx<0)for(let k=i.returnHistory.length-1;k>=0;k--){
021329:     const r=i.returnHistory[k];
021330:     if(r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay'){idx=k;break;}
021331:   }
021332:   if(idx<0)for(let k=i.returnHistory.length-1;k>=0;k--){ if(i.returnHistory[k]){idx=k;break;} }
021333:   if(idx<0){toast('No return to undo');return;}
021334:   undoReturnEntry(m,id,idx);
021335: }

021337: // ── Unified "step back one state" ───────────────────────────────────────────
021338: // One consistent ← Back button on any state past the item's base. Dispatches to
021339: // the right existing revert. (v2.21.48)
021340: function _returnedToStockDate(i){
021341:   if(!i||!i.isReturned)return null;
021342:   const _fr=(i.returnHistory||[]).slice().reverse().find(function(r){return r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay';});
021343:   return (_fr&&_fr.loggedAt)?String(_fr.loggedAt).split('T')[0]:null;
021344: }
021345: function _latestRelistedReturnRef(i){
021346:   if(!i||!Array.isArray(i.returnHistory))return null;
021347:   for(let k=i.returnHistory.length-1;k>=0;k--){
021348:     const r=i.returnHistory[k];
021349:     if(r&&(r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')&&r._relistedAt)return {r:r,index:k};
021350:   }
021351:   return null;
021352: }
021353: function _stepBackTitle(i){
021354:   if(!i)return null;
021355:   if(i.scrappedAt)return 'Restore to stock';
021356:   if(i.isReturned)return 'Back to sold';
021357:   if(i.resaleSalePrice)return 'Undo Sale '+_currentResaleSaleNo(i);
021358:   if(i.dateSold)return 'Back to active stock';
021359:   // v1.4.6 — a returned item that has been relisted is NOT a first-cycle
021360:   // listing. Stepping back must undo that relist, never withdraw it to sourced.
021361:   const rr=_latestRelistedReturnRef(i);
021362:   if(_itemLifecycleState(i)==='listed'&&rr)return 'Undo relist';
021363:   if(i.state==='listed')return 'Back to sourced';
021364:   return null;
021365: }
021366: function stepBack(m,id){
021367:   const i=(DB[m]||[]).find(function(x){return x.id===id;});
021368:   if(!i)return;
021369:   if(i.scrappedAt)return unScrap(m,id);
021370:   if(i.isReturned)return undoLastReturn(m,id);
021371:   if(i.resaleSalePrice)return undoResale(m,id);
021372:   if(i.dateSold)return revertToActive(m,id);
021373:   const rr=_latestRelistedReturnRef(i);
021374:   if(_itemLifecycleState(i)==='listed'&&rr)return undoRelist(m,id,rr.index);
021375:   if(i.state==='listed')return withdrawToSourced(m,id);
021376: }
021377: async function undoResale(m,id){
021378:   const i=(DB[m]||[]).find(function(x){return x.id===id;});
021379:   if(!i||!i.resaleSalePrice)return;
021380:   const saleNo=_currentResaleSaleNo(i);
021381:   const adjustments=(i.returnHistory||[]).filter(function(r){return (Number(r.saleNo)||1)===saleNo;}).length;
021382:   const ok=await showConfirm(
021383:     'Undo Sale '+saleNo+'?',
021384:     'This clears Sale '+saleNo+' and returns the item to its active relisted stock.'+(adjustments?' '+adjustments+' Sale '+saleNo+' refund adjustment'+(adjustments===1?'':'s')+' will also be removed because that sale will no longer exist.':'')+' The relist fee remains because that listing was actually created.',
021385:     {okLabel:'Undo sale',danger:false}
021386:   );
021387:   if(!ok)return;
021388:   i.resaleSalePrice=null;i.resaleDateSold=null;i.resaleGrossProfit=null;
021389:   // Keep i.resalePlatform and the live i.shippingCost/postage/packaging/promo
021390:   // because they are the active relist settings. Completed-sale snapshots go.
021391:   i.resalePostage=null;i.resaleShippingCost=null;i.resalePackagingCost=null;i.resalePromoPercent=null;
021392:   if(adjustments)i.returnHistory=(i.returnHistory||[]).filter(function(r){return (Number(r.saleNo)||1)!==saleNo;});
021393:   i.isReturned=false;
021394:   i.state='listed';i.grossProfit=null;
021395:   saveDB();renderItemPage(m,id);toast('Sale '+saleNo+' undone — item back to listed');
021396: }

021409: async function revertToActive(m,id){
021410:   const i=(DB[m]||[]).find(x=>x.id===id);
021411:   if(!i)return;
021412:   // Safety: only revert a sold-but-not-returned, not-resold item.
021413:   if(!i.dateSold||i.resaleSalePrice||i.isReturned)return;
021414:   const s1Adjustments=(i.returnHistory||[]).filter(function(r){return (Number(r.saleNo)||1)===1;}).length;
021415:   const ok=await showConfirm(
021416:     'Move back to active?',

021418:   );
021419:   if(!ok)return;
021420:   i.dateSold=null;
021421:   if(s1Adjustments)i.returnHistory=(i.returnHistory||[]).filter(function(r){return (Number(r.saleNo)||1)!==1;});

021425:   // its platform from defaultPlatform again (otherwise the item-page platform

021427:   i.soldOnPlatform=null;
021428:   saveDB();

021432:   if(cur==='p-item')renderItemPage(m,id);
021433:   else if(cur==='p-monthly')renderMonth();
021434:   else if(cur==='p-stock')renderStock();
021435:   else renderSummary();

021438: async function undoSale(m,id){

021440:   if(!i)return;
021441:   const s1Adjustments=(i.returnHistory||[]).filter(function(r){return (Number(r.saleNo)||1)===1;}).length;

021445:     {okLabel:'Undo sale',danger:false}

021459: function undoRelist(m,id,rtnIdx){
021460:   const i=(DB[m]||[]).find(x=>x.id===id);
021461:   if(!i||!i.returnHistory)return;
021462:   const r=i.returnHistory[rtnIdx];
021463:   if(!r||!r._relistedAt)return;
021464:   const saleNo=Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1);
021465: 
021466:   // A relist can only be undone while it is still the latest structural step.
021467:   // If the next Sale-N already completed, step that sale back first.
021468:   const _liveNo=i.resaleSalePrice?_currentResaleSaleNo(i):null;
021469:   const _laterFrozen=(i.returnHistory||[]).some(function(r2){
021470:     return r2!==r&&(r2.type==='full_seller'||r2.type==='full_ebay')&&Math.max(1,Number(r2.saleNo)||1)>saleNo;
021471:   });
021472:   if((_liveNo&&_liveNo>saleNo)||_laterFrozen){
021473:     toast('Undo the later sale first, then undo this relist.','error');
021474:     return;
021475:   }
021476: 
021477:   // Remove relist markers from this return entry. The listing fee belongs to
021478:   // the relist action itself, so a correction that undoes the relist removes
021479:   // that fee from the event ledger as well.
021480:   const _undoneDate=r._relistedAt;
021481:   delete r._relistedAt;
021482:   delete r._relistCondition;
021483:   delete r._platformAtRelist;
021484:   delete r._listingFeeAtRelist;
021485: 
021486:   // v2.15.0 — drop the paired maintenance entry, or the relist counter and the
021487:   // freshness clock would keep counting a relist that has just been undone.
021488:   if(Array.isArray(i.refreshHistory)){
021489:     const _paired=i.refreshHistory
021490:       .map(function(e,ix){return {e:e,ix:ix};})
021491:       .filter(function(o){return o.e.type==='relist'&&o.e._fromReturn&&o.e.date===_undoneDate;})
021492:       .pop();
021493:     if(_paired)i.refreshHistory.splice(_paired.ix,1);
021494:     const _prior=i.refreshHistory.slice().reverse().find(function(e){return e.type==='relist';});
021495:     i._lastRelistAt=_prior?_prior.date:null;
021496:   }
021497: 
021498:   // confirmRelist clears the reusable resale slot after Sale 2+. Rebuild that
021499:   // failed completed sale from the immutable return snapshot so a subsequent
021500:   // "Undo return" can correctly move the item back to Sale N sold state.
021501:   if(saleNo>=2){
021502:     const _num=function(a,b,fallback){
021503:       if(a!=null)return Number(a)||0;
021504:       if(b!=null)return Number(b)||0;
021505:       return Number(fallback)||0;
021506:     };
021507:     i.resaleSalePrice=_num(r._salePriceAtReturn,r._salePriceAtRelist,0);
021508:     i.resaleDateSold=r._dateSoldAtReturn||null;
021509:     i.resalePostage=_num(r._postageAtReturn,r._postageAtRelist,0);
021510:     i.resaleShippingCost=_num(r._shippingCostAtReturn,r._shippingCostAtRelist,0);
021511:     i.resalePackagingCost=_num(r._packagingCostAtReturn,r._packagingCostAtRelist,0);
021512:     i.resalePromoPercent=_num(r._promoPercentAtReturn,r._promoPercentAtRelist,0);
021513:     i.resaleListingFee=_num(r._listingFeeAtReturn,null,0);
021514:     i.resalePlatform=r._platformAtReturn||i.resalePlatform||_itemPlatform(i);
021515:     i.resaleBundleId=r._bundleIdAtReturn||null;
021516:     i.resaleBundleRef=r._bundleRefAtReturn||'';
021517:     i.resaleBundleTotal=Number(r._bundleTotalAtReturn)||0;
021518:     // Restore live fields used as defaults by the return/relist UI.
021519:     i.salePrice=i.resaleSalePrice;
021520:     i.postage=i.resalePostage;
021521:     i.shippingCost=i.resaleShippingCost;
021522:     i.packagingCost=i.resalePackagingCost;
021523:     i.promoPercent=i.resalePromoPercent;
021524:   }else{
021525:     // Sale 1 stays frozen in its original fields/snapshot; only remove the
021526:     // active resale-cycle fee that the now-undone relist had stamped.
021527:     i.resaleListingFee=0;
021528:   }
021529: 
021530:   // Restore returned state.
021531:   i.isReturned=true;
021532:   i.state='returned';
021533:   i.dateSold=null;
021534:   i.condition=undefined;
021535:   i.grossProfit=calcGrossProfit(i);
021536:   saveDB();
021537:   renderItemPage(m,id);
021538:   toast('Relist undone — item back to Returned after Sale '+saleNo);
021539: }
021540: 
021541: // ── Returned items panel (opened from KPI card) ────────────────────────────
021542: function showReturnedItems(){
021543:   // IMPORTANT: this panel must use the exact same attribution rule as the
021544:   // dashboard KPI — a refund belongs to the period in which it was LOGGED.
021545:   // Build one row per item, but only from return entries inside the selected
021546:   // period. Older/later returns on the same item must not leak into totals.
021547:   const items=[];
021548:   allDBKeys().forEach(function(k){
021549:     (DB[k]||[]).forEach(function(i){
021550:       if((i.item||'').trim().toUpperCase()==='MONTH END')return;
021551:       const periodReturns=(i.returnHistory||[]).map(function(r,idx){
021552:         return {entry:r,index:idx,date:_returnEventDate(r)};
021553:       }).filter(function(x){return x.date&&_inSummaryPeriod(x.date);});
021554:       if(!periodReturns.length)return;
021555:       items.push(Object.assign({},i,{month:k,_periodReturns:periodReturns}));
021556:     });
021557:   });
021558: 
021559:   items.sort(function(a,b){
021560:     const ad=a._periodReturns[a._periodReturns.length-1].date||'';
021561:     const bd=b._periodReturns[b._periodReturns.length-1].date||'';
021562:     return bd.localeCompare(ad);
021563:   });

022038: // (rather than the listing-month bucket the item lives in inside DB).

022040: // Build a list of sale events that landed in month `m` (sale1 + sale2, attributed
022041: // by dateSold / resaleDateSold respectively). Each entry is a SALE EVENT — an item
022042: // with both an original sale and a resale in different months will produce two
022043: // entries, one per month.
022044: //
022045: // Returns array of: {item, month (DB key the item lives in), sale ('1' or '2'),
022046: //                    saleDate, salePrice, profit, roi, daysToSell, isReturned}
022047: //
022048: // `sale1` profit/revenue uses the item's original-sale-only state — for items
022049: // that have been relisted, this means reconstructing the original sale via the
022050: // snapshot stored on the full_return entry (see _origSalePrice convention).
022051: // `sale2` uses calcGrossProfit on the full item (which already includes the
022052: // relist accounting).
022053: // Return/refund events are dated independently from the original sale.
022054: // This preserves the original sale in its sale month and records the cash impact
022055: // in the month the return/refund was actually logged.
022056: 
022057: // ── F2/F3 event-profit reconciliation ──────────────────────────────────────
022058: // The sale-1, return and sale-2 events for one item must sum to
022059: // calcGrossProfit(i) — the canonical per-item all-time gross that feeds the
022060: // Dashboard, Money and cash. Historically the sale-2 event carried the WHOLE
022061: // item's gross (which already includes the sale-1 leg + the returns), so a
022062: // resold-after-return item double-counted; separately a fully-returned or
022063: // partially-refunded item's events missed the promoted-listing boost credit
022064: // and the partial-refund fee credit that calcGrossProfit applies. Fix: give
022065: // each event a BASE contribution (sale-1 = clean snapshot gross, return = its
022066: // own cash impact, sale-2 = 0) and route the whole residual onto the item's
022067: // TERMINAL event (sale-2 if resold, else the latest-dated return, else sale-1).
022068: // The set then sums to calcGrossProfit(i) by construction, for every shape.
022069: // DISPLAY-ONLY: the SA103/tax path (_buildPnLSummary → _saleBreakdown) reads
022070: // per-leg breakdowns and never reads these .profit fields.
022071: 
022072: 
022073: // v2.21.15 — The resale (sale 2) valued on its OWN leg: resale price with the
022074: // resale's shipping/packaging/promo and the item cost SUNK (0, paid in cycle 1).
022075: // Mirrors _sale1EventSnapshot. calcGrossProfit(this) == the item page's Sale 2
022076: // net, so the resale month shows honest resale economics.
022077: 
022078: // Fix B (B1): the Sales-History screen derives its month universe from item
022079: // STORAGE keys (purchase months). But events are attributed by SALE/RETURN

022220: // the loss-so-far from the failed first sale and isn't useful as an "estimate"

022230: // the same net profit used everywhere else (calcGrossProfit). Mirrors

022656: function fmt(n){
022657:   if(n===null||n===undefined)return'£0.00';
022658:   // E3 P2 #8 — Add thousands separator so large values render as
022659:   // £1,234.56 not £1234.56. Negative values keep minus prefix
022660:   // before the £ sign for readability ("-£1,234.56").
022661:   const num=Number(n);
022662:   if(!isFinite(num))return'£0.00';
022663:   const abs=Math.abs(num).toFixed(2);
022664:   const parts=abs.split('.');
022665:   parts[0]=parts[0].replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g,',');
022666:   return(num<0?'-£':'£')+parts.join('.');
022667: }

022671:   const d=new Date(ds+'T12:00:00');
022672:   return d.toLocaleDateString('en-GB',{day:'2-digit',month:'short',year:'numeric'});
022673: }

022679:   return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;').replace(/'/g,'&#39;');
022680: }

022703:   // the display — eBay Biz FVF is category-rated, so the stored profit (used in
022704:   // Summary totals, monthly P&L and tax) would otherwise stay stale.
022705:   if(field==='category'&&i.dateSold){

022710:   // category matters because eBay Biz FVF is category-rated — without this the
022711:   // fee label, BPF and Net Profit wouldn't refresh until the next full re-render.

022714:     if(pg&&pg.id==='p-item')renderItemPage(m,id);
